Static-analysis context for repository-level code navigation. Use the source links to verify details. Call edges are analyzer-resolved relationships, not complete runtime traces.

## Analyzer Architecture Summary

- trustyai-service-operator (also known as\: TrustyAI operator) is a Kubernetes component.
- managing 3 CRD(s)\: EvalHub, LMEvalJob, TrustyAIService.
- deploying 1 workload(s) (trustyai-service-operator-controller-manager) with 1 container(s).
- watching 17 resource type(s).
- exposing 3 service(s) on ports\: 443, 8443.
- protected by 3 network policy/policies.
- runtime dependencies\: OpenTelemetry Collector.
- defining 26 API spec type(s) across 78 struct(s) with 332 total fields.
- with 610 operator config constants (33 image references, 7 env vars).
- following 27 reconciliation steps across 4 controller(s).
- reporting 15 status condition types.
- integrates with Istio, KServe, Kueue, management relationship via labels/annotations.
- deploys kube-rbac-proxy sidecar for RBAC-based authorization.
- kustomize overlays\: config/base, config/components/evalhub, config/components/job-mgr, config/components/lmes, config/components/nemo-guardrails, config/components/tas, config/components/webhook, config/configmaps/evalhub, config/configmaps, config/configmaps/nemo-guardrails, config/crd, config/manager, config/manifests, config/overlays/evalhub-only, config/overlays/lmes, config/overlays/mcp-guardrails, config/overlays/odh, config/overlays/odh/rbac/tls, config/overlays/odh-kueue, config/overlays/rhoai, config/overlays/rhoai/rbac/tls, config/overlays/testing, config/prometheus, config/rbac-base, config/scorecard.
- references components\: eval-hub (import, provider), kube-rbac-proxy (config).
